 15203:         {
 15204:             builder->failure_message = S8("a compiler runtime call argument has an incompatible canonical type");
 15205:             emitted = false;
 15206:         }
 15207:     }
 15208:     for (u32 symbol_index = 0; emitted && symbol.value == IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID && symbol_index < builder->program->symbols.count; symbol_index += 1)

 15212:         {
 15213:             if (candidate->kind != IR_SYMBOL_FUNCTION)
 15214:             {
 15215:                 builder->failure_message = S8("a compiler runtime symbol conflicts with a non-function declaration");
 15216:                 emitted = false;
 15217:             }
 15218:             else
 15219:             {

 24613: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL IrValueId c_ir_emit_zero_value(CIntegerIrBuilder* builder, IrTypeId root_type, CToken token)
 24614: {
 24615:     typedef enum CIrZeroTaskKind
 24616:     {
 24617:         C_IR_ZERO_TASK_VALUE,
 24618:         C_IR_ZERO_TASK_COMPLETE,
 24619:     } CIrZeroTaskKind;
 24620:     typedef struct CIrZeroTask CIrZeroTask;
 24621:     struct CIrZeroTask
 24622:     {
 24623:         IrValueId* output;
 24624:         IrValueId* operands;
 24625:         u64* fields;
 24626:         IrTypeId type;
 24627:         u32 operand_count;
 24628:         CIrZeroTaskKind kind;
 24629:     };
 24630:     IrType* root = ir_type_from_id(&builder->program->types, root_type);
 24631:     if (!root || !root->layout.resolved || root->layout.size > (UINT32_MAX - 64) / 8)
 24632:     {
 24633:         builder->failure_message = !root                    ? S8("cannot zero-initialize an unresolved IR type")
 24634:                                    : !root->layout.resolved ? S8("cannot zero-initialize an aggregate with unresolved layout")
 24635:                                                             : S8("aggregate is too large to zero-initialize");
 24636:         return IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
 24637:     }
 24638:     u32 capacity = (u32)(root->layout.size * 8 + 64);
 24639:     CIrZeroTask* tasks = arena_allocate(builder->temporary_arena, CIrZeroTask, capacity);
 24640:     IrValueId result = IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
 24641:     u32 task_count = 1;
 24642:     tasks[0] = (CIrZeroTask){
 24643:         .output = &result,
 24644:         .type = root_type,
 24645:     };
 24646:     while (task_count)
 24647:     {
 24648:         CIrZeroTask task = tasks[--task_count];
 24649:         IrType* type = ir_type_from_id(&builder->program->types, task.type);
 24650:         if (!type)
 24651:         {
 24652:             builder->failure_message = string_format(builder->arena, S8("cannot zero-initialize unresolved IR type {u32}"), task.type.value);
 24653:             return IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
 24654:         }
 24655:         if (task.kind == C_IR_ZERO_TASK_COMPLETE)
 24656:         {
 24657:             if (type->kind != IR_TYPE_ARRAY && type->kind != IR_TYPE_VECTOR && task.operand_count > UINT16_MAX)
 24658:             {
 24659:                 builder->failure_message = string_format(builder->arena, S8("aggregate has too many fields to lower ({u32})"), task.operand_count);
 24660:                 return IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
 24661:             }
 24662:             IrSourceRange instruction_source = c_ir_token_source_range(builder, token);
 24663:             IrValueId aggregate = c_ir_emit_initializer_capture(builder, task.type, task.operands, task.fields, task.operand_count, instruction_source);
 24664:             if (aggregate.value == IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID)
 24665:             {
 24666:                 return IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
 24667:             }
 24668:             *task.output = aggregate;
 24669:             continue;
 24670:         }
 24671:         bool aggregate = type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY || type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR || type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION;
 24672:         if (!aggregate)
 24673:         {
 24674:             IrValueId zero = IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
 24675:             if (type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
 24676:             {
 24677:                 if (type->bit_width == 80)
 24678:                 {
 24679:                     // The f80 constant carries the semantic ten bytes as two
 24680:                     // integer immediates; do not route synthetic zero through
 24681:                     // a host f64 literal or a generic floating conversion.
 24682:                     zero = c_ir_emit_f80_constant_bits(builder, c_ir_token_source_range(builder, token), S8("0.0L"), 0, 0);
 24683:                 }
 24684:                 else
 24685:                 {
 24686:                     CToken zero_token = {
 24687:                         .offset = C_SPELLING_FLOAT_ZERO,
 24688:                         .length = type->bit_width == 32 ? 4u : 3u,
 24689:                         .kind = C_TOKEN_PREPROCESSING_NUMBER,
 24690:                     };
 24691:                     zero = c_ir_emit_float(builder, zero_token);
 24692:                     zero = c_ir_emit_cast(builder, zero, task.type, c_ir_token_source_range(builder, token));
 24693:                 }
 24694:             }
 24695:             else if (type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
 24696:             {
 24697:                 zero = c_ir_emit_integer_value_typed(builder, 0, false, token, task.type);
 24698:             }
 24699:             else if (type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER)
 24700:             {
 24701:                 zero = c_ir_emit_integer_value(builder, 0, false, token);
 24702:                 zero = c_ir_emit_cast(builder, zero, task.type, c_ir_token_source_range(builder, token));
 24703:             }
 24704:             else if (type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN)
 24705:             {
 24706:                 zero = c_ir_emit_integer_value(builder, 0, false, token);
 24707:                 zero = c_ir_truth_value(builder, zero, c_ir_token_source_range(builder, token));
 24708:             }
 24709:             if (zero.value == IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID)
 24710:             {
 24711:                 builder->failure_message = string_format(builder->arena, S8("cannot zero-initialize IR type kind {u32}"), (u32)type->kind);
 24712:                 return zero;
 24713:             }
 24714:             *task.output = zero;
 24715:             continue;
 24716:         }
 24717:         u64 child_count_u64 = (type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY || type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR) ? type->element_count
 24718:                               : type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION                                 ? (type->field_count ? 1 : 0)
 24719:                                                                                             : type->field_count;
 24720:         if (child_count_u64 > UINT32_MAX || child_count_u64 > capacity - task_count - 1)
 24721:         {
 24722:             builder->failure_message =

 24724:             return IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
 24725:         }
 24726:         u32 child_count = (u32)child_count_u64;
 24727:         IrValueId* operands = arena_allocate(builder->arena, IrValueId, child_count);
 24728:         u64* fields = (type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY || type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR) ? 0 : arena_allocate(builder->arena, u64, child_count);
 24729:         tasks[task_count++] = (CIrZeroTask){
 24730:             .output = task.output,
 24731:             .operands = operands,
 24732:             .fields = fields,
 24733:             .type = task.type,
 24734:             .operand_count = child_count,
 24735:             .kind = C_IR_ZERO_TASK_COMPLETE,
 24736:         };
 24737:         for (u32 child_index = child_count; child_index; child_index -= 1)
 24738:         {

   665: typedef enum CTypeKind
   666: {
   667:     C_TYPE_INVALID,
   668:     C_TYPE_VOID,
   669:     C_TYPE_BOOL,
   670:     C_TYPE_CHAR,
   671:     C_TYPE_SIGNED_CHAR,
   672:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_CHAR,
   673:     C_TYPE_SHORT,
   674:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_SHORT,
   675:     C_TYPE_INT,
   676:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_INT,
   677:     C_TYPE_LONG,
   678:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_LONG,
   679:     C_TYPE_LONG_LONG,
   680:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
   681:     C_TYPE_INT128,
   682:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_INT128,
   683:     // IEEE-754 binary16, spelled `_Float16`.  It is a real type, not a
   684:     // storage-only alias: it has its own two-byte layout, its own rank below
   685:     // `float` in the usual arithmetic conversions, and its own place in the
   686:     // vector element ladder.  Clang's `avx512fp16intrin.h` declares
   687:     // `__m512h` out of it, which is what brought it in.
   688:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16,
   689:     C_TYPE_BFLOAT16,
   690:     C_TYPE_FLOAT,
   691:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE,
   692:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE,
   693:     // The three C99 complex types and the `_Float16 _Complex` extension
   694:     // beside them. Each is laid out as two contiguous elements of its
   695:     // underlying real type -- real part first -- which is both what the
   696:     // psABIs specify and what lets the IR model them as two-field
   697:     // aggregates (see c_ir_scalar_type). Clang's `_mm512_set1_pch` takes a
   698:     // `_Float16 _Complex` parameter, which is what brought the half form in.
   699:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX,
   700:     C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX,
   701:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   702:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   703:     C_TYPE_VA_LIST,
   704:     C_TYPE_NULLPTR,
   705:     C_TYPE_POINTER,
   706:     C_TYPE_ARRAY,
   707:     C_TYPE_VECTOR,
   708:     C_TYPE_FUNCTION,
   709:     C_TYPE_STRUCT,
   710:     C_TYPE_UNION,
   711:     C_TYPE_ENUM,
   712:     C_TYPE_COUNT,
   713: } CTypeKind;
   714: 
   715: // The complex kinds and their underlying real kind, in both directions. Every
   716: // complex question in the frontend goes through these three so the mapping
   717: // lives in one place; `c_type_kind_complex_of` returns C_TYPE_INVALID for a
   718: // real kind that has no complex counterpart (C only defines the three).
   719: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE bool c_type_kind_is_complex(CTypeKind kind)

   693:     // The three C99 complex types and the `_Float16 _Complex` extension
   694:     // beside them. Each is laid out as two contiguous elements of its
   695:     // underlying real type -- real part first -- which is both what the
   696:     // psABIs specify and what lets the IR model them as two-field
   697:     // aggregates (see c_ir_scalar_type). Clang's `_mm512_set1_pch` takes a
   698:     // `_Float16 _Complex` parameter, which is what brought the half form in.
   699:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX,
   700:     C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX,
   701:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   702:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   703:     C_TYPE_VA_LIST,
   704:     C_TYPE_NULLPTR,
   705:     C_TYPE_POINTER,
   706:     C_TYPE_ARRAY,
   707:     C_TYPE_VECTOR,
   708:     C_TYPE_FUNCTION,
   709:     C_TYPE_STRUCT,
   710:     C_TYPE_UNION,
   711:     C_TYPE_ENUM,
   712:     C_TYPE_COUNT,
   713: } CTypeKind;
   714: 
   715: // The complex kinds and their underlying real kind, in both directions. Every
   716: // complex question in the frontend goes through these three so the mapping
   717: // lives in one place; `c_type_kind_complex_of` returns C_TYPE_INVALID for a
   718: // real kind that has no complex counterpart (C only defines the three).
   719: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE bool c_type_kind_is_complex(CTypeKind kind)
   720: {
   721:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX;
   722: }

   757: typedef struct CType CType;
   758: struct CType
   759: {
   760:     String8 tag;
   761:     // The scope a tagged aggregate was declared in, so two block-scope
   762:     // definitions of one tag in sibling functions stay two types -- clang's
   763:     // xmmintrin.h defines `struct __mm_storeh_pi_struct` inside two
   764:     // intrinsics -- and a reference resolves to the innermost visible one.
   765:     // Meaningful only for struct/union/enum types with a tag; everything
   766:     // else leaves it zero, which is the file scope.
   767:     CScopeId tag_scope;
   768:     CTypeId element_type;
   769:     CTypeId return_type;
   770:     CTypeId unqualified_type;
   771:     u32 array_bound;
   772:     u32 parameter_start;
   773:     u32 parameter_count;
   774:     u32 member_start;
   775:     u32 member_count;
   776:     u32 enum_member_start;
   777:     u32 enum_member_count;
   778:     u32 definition_start;
   779:     u32 definition_token_count;
   780:     u32 vector_byte_size;
   781:     CTypeKind kind;
   782:     bool is_const;
   783:     bool is_volatile;
   784:     bool is_restrict;
   785:     bool is_atomic;
   786:     bool is_variadic;
   787:     bool is_complete;
   788:     // GNU's transparent_union: a parameter of this union type accepts an
   789:     // argument of any member's type, passed as that member's own bits.
   790:     bool is_transparent_union;
   791:     bool has_unqualified_type;
   792:     // The function type was written `()`, with no parameter list at all --
   793:     // not `(void)`, which declares zero parameters. C11 6.2.7p3 makes an
   794:     // unprototyped declaration compatible with a non-variadic prototype, so
   795:     // musl's `long __syscall_cp_asm();` and the eight-parameter prototype
   796:     // beside it declare one function rather than two conflicting ones.
   797:     bool is_unprototyped;
   798:     // For enums, element_type is the resolved compatible integer type. This
   799:     // flag distinguishes a written fixed base from ordinary range selection.
   800:     bool has_fixed_underlying_type;
   801: };

   823: // One `_Alignas(...)` or GNU `aligned(...)` request, as either the type it
   824: // names or the token range of its constant expression. Which of the two
   825: // spellings wrote it is not stored: token_start is always two tokens past the
   826: // keyword -- the keyword and its `(` -- so c_alignment_specifier_is_standard
   827: // reads it back out of the token stream, and this table is sized at one record
   828: // per token of the translation unit, where a flag word would commit four more
   829: // bytes per token for a fact only a rejected request ever asks about.

   881: // Integer constant-expression facts retained before enum compatible-type
   882: // selection. `magnitude_high:magnitude` is an unsigned 128-bit mathematical
   883: // magnitude; `is_negative` supplies its sign independently of the expression's
   884: // C type. `type`, `rank`, `bit_width`, and `is_signed` describe the resolved
   885: // semantic integer type after literal typing, casts, promotions, and the usual
   886: // arithmetic conversions. Explicit initializers retain their original ICE
   887: // type; implicit enumerators retain the value and type of their successor step.

   917: struct CEnumMember
   918: {
   919:     String8 name;
   920:     CSourceLocation location;
   921:     // Interned id of `name`; zero when parsing without a symbol table.
   922:     u32 symbol;
   923:     // The source token and owning enum retain declaration order and scope for
   924:     // pending lookup, before file-scope ordinary entities are published.
   925:     u32 token_index;
   926:     CTypeId enum_type;
   927:     // `declaration_type` never changes. `type` is initially that type and is
   928:     // finalized once, at the closing brace, according to the selected dialect.
   929:     CTypeId declaration_type;
   930:     CTypeId type;
   931:     CIntegerConstant integer_constant;
   932:     u64 value;
   933:     bool is_negative;
   934:     // Once published, ordinary lookup is authoritative; this avoids scanning
   935:     // completed lists for unresolved non-enum identifiers and keywords.
   936:     bool is_published;
   937:     u8 reserved[6];
   938: };
   939: BUSTER_CT_CHECK(sizeof(CEnumMember) == 104);

   887: // type; implicit enumerators retain the value and type of their successor step.

   916: typedef struct CEnumMember CEnumMember;
   917: struct CEnumMember
   918: {
   919:     String8 name;
   920:     CSourceLocation location;
   921:     // Interned id of `name`; zero when parsing without a symbol table.
   922:     u32 symbol;
   923:     // The source token and owning enum retain declaration order and scope for
   924:     // pending lookup, before file-scope ordinary entities are published.
   925:     u32 token_index;
   926:     CTypeId enum_type;
   927:     // `declaration_type` never changes. `type` is initially that type and is
   928:     // finalized once, at the closing brace, according to the selected dialect.
   929:     CTypeId declaration_type;
   930:     CTypeId type;
   931:     CIntegerConstant integer_constant;
   932:     u64 value;
   933:     bool is_negative;
   934:     // Once published, ordinary lookup is authoritative; this avoids scanning
   935:     // completed lists for unresolved non-enum identifiers and keywords.
   936:     bool is_published;
   937:     u8 reserved[6];
   938: };
   939: BUSTER_CT_CHECK(sizeof(CEnumMember) == 104);

   966: typedef struct CEntity CEntity;
   967: struct CEntity
   968: {
   969:     String8 name;
   970:     // Interned id of `name` (0 when the parse ran without a symbol table);
   971:     // the scope-lookup buckets and chains key on it.
   972:     u32 symbol;
   973:     CSourceLocation location;
   974:     CTypeId type;
   975:     CScopeId scope;
   976:     CEntityId next_in_scope;
   977:     CEntityId next_in_lookup;
   978:     CEntityId next_typedef_in_lookup;
   979:     CEntityId next_by_name;
   980:     u32 declaration_index;
   981:     u32 declaration_token_plus_one;
   982:     // First token of the complete block-scope declaration. Local declarator
   983:     // ranges below deliberately exclude shared specifiers.
   984:     u32 declaration_statement_start;
   985:     u32 declaration_token_start;
   986:     u32 declaration_token_count;
   987:     u32 alignment_start;
   988:     u32 alignment_count;
   989:     CEntityKind kind;
   990:     bool is_definition;
   991:     bool is_static_storage;
   992:     bool is_thread_local;
   993:     bool is_constexpr;
   994:     bool has_constant_value;
   995:     bool constant_is_negative;
   996:     bool has_cleanup;
   997:     bool cleanup_attribute_checked;
   998:     // Register storage on a local. Only the local declarator path records it,
   999:     // and only one reader needs it: an assembler label on a `register` local
  1000:     // binds a machine register instead of renaming a symbol.
  1001:     bool is_register;
  1002:     // Block-scope extern declarations are C_ENTITY_LOCAL for lexical lookup,
  1003:     // but they name external storage rather than an automatic local place.
  1004:     bool is_extern;
  1005:     // The function's only definition so far is GNU inline-only, so the unit
  1006:     // may still give its external definition.
  1007:     bool definition_is_gnu_inline_only;
  1008:     CEntityId cleanup_function;
  1009:     u32 cleanup_attribute_token;
  1010:     u32 cleanup_attribute_end;
  1011:     // One-based member index for enumerators, zero otherwise. Wide enum values
  1012:     // stay in their existing sparse member records, not in every entity.
  1013:     u32 enum_member_plus_one;
  1014:     u64 constant_value;
  1015: };

  1054: typedef struct CDeclaration CDeclaration;
  1055: struct CDeclaration
  1056: {
  1057:     String8 name;
  1058:     CSourceLocation location;
  1059:     u32 token_start;
  1060:     u32 token_count;
  1061:     // The one declarator this declaration owns out of a comma-separated list,
  1062:     // initializer included and the separating ',' or ';' excluded. Zero count
  1063:     // means the declaration was not split and owns its whole token range;
  1064:     // token_start/token_count always span the declaration specifiers, which
  1065:     // every declarator of the list shares.
  1066:     u32 declarator_start;
  1067:     u32 declarator_count;
  1068:     u32 body_start;
  1069:     u32 body_token_count;
  1070:     u32 parameter_start;
  1071:     u32 parameter_count;
  1072:     u32 alignment_start;
  1073:     u32 alignment_count;
  1074:     CTypeId type;
  1075:     CTypeId base_type;
  1076:     CEntityId entity;
  1077:     CScopeId scope;
  1078:     CParserDeclaration* syntax_declaration;
  1079:     CDeclarationKind kind;
  1080:     bool is_definition;
  1081:     bool is_variadic;
  1082:     bool is_constexpr;
  1083:     // A GNU `extern inline` function definition: its body is only for
  1084:     // inlining, so it defines no symbol (c_ir_declaration_is_gnu_inline_only).
  1085:     bool is_gnu_inline_only;
  1086:     // Set on the second and later declarators of a list: the specifiers were
  1087:     // already parsed for the first one, so base_type is supplied rather than
  1088:     // recomputed.
  1089:     bool is_declarator_continuation;
  1090: };

   166: // Whether the statement at `index` is the substatement a control statement is
   167: // waiting for. Shared by the named-label proof below and by the switch body
   168: // scan, which must not split a case range at a label standing there.
   169: BUSTER_C_EXTERN bool c_ir_control_substatement_position(CPreprocessResult const* preprocess, u32 body_start, u32 index);
   170: // The cold half of c_ir_named_label_at: the full proof, reached only for a
   171: // token that already looks like `<identifier> :`.
   172: BUSTER_C_EXTERN bool c_ir_named_label_proven_at(CPreprocessResult const* preprocess, u32 body_start, u32 index, u32 body_end);
   173: // Whether a named label starts at `index`. Both loops that size and fill a
   174: // body's label table ask this of every body token, so the necessary condition
   175: // — an identifier followed by a colon — is inline and the proof stays out of
   176: // line: the answer is almost always no, and an out-of-line call per body
   177: // token to reach it is the expensive part. The three range tests come first
   178: // because they are what makes the two token reads in bounds.
   179: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE bool c_ir_named_label_at(CPreprocessResult const* preprocess, u32 body_start, u32 index,
   180:                                                                              u32 body_end)
   181: {
   182:     return body_end <= preprocess->token_count && index < body_end && index + 1 < body_end &&
   183:            preprocess->tokens[index].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && preprocess->tokens[index + 1].punctuator == C_PUNCTUATOR_COLON &&
   184:            c_ir_named_label_proven_at(preprocess, body_start, index, body_end);
   185: }

   187:                                                                   u32 start, u32 end, CIrDecodedString* decoded_out);
   188: // The same answer without the bytes, for the callers that only size or type
   189: // the literal; see the definition.
   190: BUSTER_C_EXTERN bool c_ir_count_string_literal_range_for_target(Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess, Target target,
   191:                                                                  u32 start, u32 end, CIrDecodedString* decoded_out);

   829: typedef struct CTypeLayoutCache CTypeLayoutCache;
   830: struct CTypeLayoutCache
   831: {
   832:     u64* sizes;
   833:     u32* alignments;
   834:     u8* states;
   835:     // Every type id whose layout is not committed in `states`, which is the
   836:     // set c_parse_type_layout's seed, solve and commit passes have anything
   837:     // to do for.  Without it each of those three passes walked the whole
   838:     // type table -- 32.141 ids on average for the 39 uncached queries of a
   839:     // stage-1 compile of this tree, for the ~6.200 that were still open.
   840:     // `pending_mark` keeps the list free of duplicates when a record
   841:     // mutation drops an id back onto it, and `pending_seeded` is the
   842:     // high-water mark of ids ever listed, so a query only has to list the
   843:     // types added since the last one.
   844:     u32* pending;
   845:     u8* pending_mark;
   846:     u32 pending_count;
   847:     u32 pending_seeded;
   848:     u32 capacity;
   849:     CToken const* tokens;
   850: };
   851: 
   852: // Successful expression facts are reused only after all bindings are final,
   853: // within one function. Scope, token end and checking mode are part of the key;
   854: // failed/speculative queries and queries over copied models are never stored.
   855: #define C_PARSE_EXPRESSION_QUERY_VALID 1u
   856: #define C_PARSE_EXPRESSION_QUERY_CHECKED 2u
   857: #define C_PARSE_EXPRESSION_QUERY_RUNTIME 4u
   858: #define C_PARSE_EXPRESSION_QUERY_CONSTANT 8u
   859:

   829: typedef struct CTypeLayoutCache CTypeLayoutCache;
   830: struct CTypeLayoutCache
   831: {

   833:     u32* alignments;
   834:     u8* states;

   836:     // set c_parse_type_layout's seed, solve and commit passes have anything
   837:     // to do for.  Without it each of those three passes walked the whole
   838:     // type table -- 32.141 ids on average for the 39 uncached queries of a
   839:     // stage-1 compile of this tree, for the ~6.200 that were still open.
   840:     // `pending_mark` keeps the list free of duplicates when a record
   841:     // mutation drops an id back onto it, and `pending_seeded` is the
   842:     // high-water mark of ids ever listed, so a query only has to list the
   843:     // types added since the last one.
   844:     u32* pending;

 14188: 
 14189: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_types_compatible_core(Arena* result_arena, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 14190:                                                        CTypeId left, CTypeId right, bool ignore_nested_qualifiers)
 14191: {
 14192:     Arena* conflicts[] = {
 14193:         result_arena,
 14194:     };
 14195:     TemporalArena temporary = scratch_begin(conflicts, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(conflicts));
 14196:     CTypePair* stack = arena_allocate(temporary.arena, CTypePair, result->type_count * 2 + 1);
 14197:     u32 stack_count = 0;
 14198:     stack[stack_count++] = (CTypePair){
 14199:         .left = left,
 14200:         .right = right,
 14201:     };
 14202:     bool compatible = true;
 14203:     while (stack_count)
 14204:     {
 14205:         CTypePair pair = stack[--stack_count];
 14206:         if (pair.left.value >= result->type_count || pair.right.value >= result->type_count)
 14207:         {
 14208:             compatible = false;
 14209:             break;
 14210:         }
 14211:         CType left_type = result->types[pair.left.value];
 14212:         CType right_type = result->types[pair.right.value];
 14213:         if (left_type.kind == C_TYPE_ENUM && right_type.kind != C_TYPE_ENUM && c_parse_expression_integer_kind(right_type.kind))
 14214:         {
 14215:             left_type.kind = c_parse_expression_value_kind(result, pair.left);
 14216:         }
 14217:         else if (right_type.kind == C_TYPE_ENUM && left_type.kind != C_TYPE_ENUM && c_parse_expression_integer_kind(left_type.kind))
 14218:         {
 14219:             right_type.kind = c_parse_expression_value_kind(result, pair.right);
 14220:         }
 14221:         if (left_type.kind != right_type.kind ||
 14222:             (!pair.ignore_qualifiers && !ignore_nested_qualifiers && (left_type.is_const != right_type.is_const || left_type.is_volatile != right_type.is_volatile ||
 14223:                                         left_type.is_restrict != right_type.is_restrict || left_type.is_atomic != right_type.is_atomic)))
 14224:         {
 14225:             compatible = false;
 14226:             break;
 14227:         }
 14228:         switch (left_type.kind)
 14229:         {
 14230:         case C_TYPE_POINTER:
 14231:         {
 14232:             stack[stack_count++] = (CTypePair){
 14233:                 .left = left_type.element_type,
 14234:                 .right = right_type.element_type,
 14235:             };
 14236:             break;
 14237:         }
 14238:         case C_TYPE_ARRAY:
 14239:         {
 14240:             if (left_type.array_bound >= result->array_bound_count || right_type.array_bound >= result->array_bound_count)
 14241:             {
 14242:                 compatible = false;
 14243:                 break;
 14244:             }
 14245:             CArrayBound left_bound = result->array_bounds[left_type.array_bound];
 14246:             CArrayBound right_bound = result->array_bounds[right_type.array_bound];
 14247:             // C11 6.7.6.2p4: `[*]` is a variable-length array of unspecified
 14248:             // size, valid only in a prototype that is not a definition -- it
 14249:             // is the standard way to forward-declare the definition that
 14250:             // spells the same parameter `[n]`. Its bound is one `*` token, so
 14251:             // the spelling comparison below saw `*` against `n`, failed to
 14252:             // evaluate `*` as a constant, and reported the pair as conflicting
 14253:             // declarations (#825). An unspecified size agrees with any bound.
 14254:             if (!left_bound.is_star && !right_bound.is_star && left_bound.token_count && right_bound.token_count)
 14255:             {
 14256:                 bool spelled_alike = left_bound.token_count == right_bound.token_count;
 14257:                 for (u32 token_index = 0; spelled_alike && token_index < left_bound.token_count; token_index += 1)
 14258:                 {
 14259:                     spelled_alike = string_equal(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[left_bound.token_start + token_index]),
 14260:                                                  c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[right_bound.token_start + token_index]));
 14261:                 }
 14262:                 // Two bounds that are spelled differently can still be the same
 14263:                 // length: DoomGeneric's tables.h declares `finetangent` with
 14264:                 // `[FINEANGLES/2]` and tables.c defines it with `[4096]`, which
 14265:                 // C requires to be one type. The spelling comparison above is
--- src/buster/lib/compiler/frontend/c/c_parse.c:14182 ---
 14177:     return c_parse_validate_constexpr_declaration(0, arena, result, preprocess, declaration);
 14178: }
 14179: #endif

    50: 
    51: // What an IR instruction remembers about where it came from: the source it
    52: // belongs to, and the byte offset and length of its spelling in the byte
    53: // space the producing frontend maps (see IrSourceMap). Line and column are
    54: // deliberately absent — recovering them costs a search per query, and the
    55: // number of ranges a lowering builds is orders of magnitude larger than the
    56: // number a compile ever asks a line of. They resolve through
    57: // `ir_source_position` at the only four places that need them: diagnostic
    58: // formatting, DWARF line-table generation, CodeView line generation, and
    59: // source-navigation requests.
    60: typedef struct IrSourceRange IrSourceRange;
    61: struct IrSourceRange
    62: {
    63:     IrSourceId source;
    64:     u32 offset;
    65:     u32 length;
    66: };
    67: 
    68: // A range is copied by value through the whole lowering surface and stored
    69: // once per instruction, so its size is part of the design, not an accident.
    70: BUSTER_CT_CHECK(sizeof(IrSourceRange) == 12);
    71: 
    72: // One range resolved: the source it lands in, the byte offset within that
    73: // source's own text, and the one-based line and column there. `line == 0`
    74: // means the range carried no position — an unresolvable offset, a frontend
    75: // that published no map, or a range that was never filled in.
    76: typedef struct IrSourcePosition IrSourcePosition;
    77: struct IrSourcePosition
    78: {
    79:     u32 source;
    80:     u32 offset;
    81:     u32 line;
    82:     u32 column;
    83: };
    84: 
    85: // A retained line start: the offset in the source's own text where a line
    86: // begins, and the line/column there. Offset and column advance one per byte
    87: // from a checkpoint until the next one, so a checkpoint is needed only where
    88: // that linearity breaks — a newline, or a byte the frontend deleted while
    89: // translating (a C line splice, a stripped carriage return).
    90: typedef struct IrSourceCheckpoint IrSourceCheckpoint;
    91: struct IrSourceCheckpoint
    92: {
    93:     u32 offset;
    94:     u32 line;
    95:     u32 column;
    96: };
    97: 
    98: typedef enum IrSourceRegionKind
    99: {
   100:     // Offsets resolve through the checkpoints, shifted by `line_delta`.

   238:     IR_ABI_CLASS_AGGREGATE,
   239:     IR_ABI_CLASS_MEMORY,
   240:     // System V x86-64 uses this pair for an 80-bit x87 value.  The upper
   241:     // eightbyte is padding/storage rather than a second independent value,
   242:     // but keeping the X87_UP class explicit lets aggregate postprocessing
   243:     // distinguish a valid long-double result from an incompatible merge.

   245:     IR_ABI_CLASS_X87_UP,

   247:     // one VECTOR part before publishing an IrAbiValue to backend consumers.

   249:     IR_ABI_CLASS_COUNT,

   293: typedef struct IrTypeLayout IrTypeLayout;
   294: struct IrTypeLayout
   295: {
   296:     u64 size;
   297:     u32 alignment;
   298:     // Legacy descriptive layout class, initialized with the language layout.
   299:     // ABI decomposition never reads or mutates this field; active convention
   300:     // decisions belong to IrAbiContext.
   301:     IrAbiClass abi_class;
   302:     bool resolved;
   303:     // The alignment the type would have if nothing had *lowered* it, or zero
   304:     // when nothing did -- which is every type but the alias a
   305:     // `typedef int pair __attribute__((aligned(2)))` makes. `alignment` is
   306:     // where the object goes; this is what the System V argument classifier
   307:     // asks about, because "contains unaligned fields" there means unaligned
   308:     // for the field's own natural alignment: Clang passes
   309:     // `struct { char tag; pair value; }` in memory even though `value` sits at
   310:     // the two-byte boundary its type asked for. One byte is enough for every
   311:     // alignment the classifier can reach, since it gives up above sixteen
   312:     // bytes of object.
   313:     u8 natural_alignment;
   314:     u8 reserved[2];
   315: };
   316: 
   317: typedef struct IrField IrField;
   318: struct IrField
   319: {
   320:     String8 name;
   321:     IrSourceRange source;
   322:     IrTypeId type;
   323:     u64 offset;
   324:     u32 bit_offset;
   325:     u32 bit_width;
   326:     bool is_bit_field;
   327:     // The bytes a bit-field is read and written through, when packing left
   328:     // the aggregate too small for a unit of the field's declared type:
   329:     // `struct __attribute__((packed)) { char c; int b : 5; char t; }` is
   330:     // three bytes, so `b` is read through the byte at offset one. Zero means
   331:     // the declared type's size, which is what every other field uses;
   332:     // `ir_field_access_size` resolves the two. The span need not be a power
   333:     // of two -- `union __attribute__((packed)) { long long b : 40; }` is five
   334:     // bytes and no unit of any width fits inside it -- in which case the
   335:     // access is the sequence `ir_field_access_pieces` decomposes it into.
   336:     u8 access_size;
   337:     u8 reserved[6];
   338: };
   339: 
   340: // One power-of-two access of a bit-field's storage, at `offset` bytes from the
   341: // field's own offset.
   342: typedef struct IrFieldAccessPiece IrFieldAccessPiece;
   343: struct IrFieldAccessPiece
   344: {
   345:     u8 offset;
   346:     u8 size;
   347: };
   348: 
   349: // A bit-field spans at most nine bytes -- sixty-four bits starting seven bits
   350: // into one. The first descending power-of-two decomposition that needs four
   351: // pieces is fifteen (eight plus four plus two plus one); seven is the largest
   352: // admitted decomposition, with four plus two plus one, that needs three.
   353: #define IR_FIELD_ACCESS_MAX_SIZE 9
   354: #define IR_FIELD_ACCESS_PIECE_CAPACITY 3
   355: BUSTER_CT_CHECK(IR_FIELD_ACCESS_MAX_SIZE < 8 + 4 + 2 + 1 && IR_FIELD_ACCESS_PIECE_CAPACITY >= 3);
   356: 
   357: typedef struct IrEnumMember IrEnumMember;
   358: struct IrEnumMember
   359: {
   360:     String8 name;
   361:     IrSourceRange source;
   362:     u64 value;
   363: };
   364:

   375:     IrTypeId unqualified_type;
   376:     IrTypeLayout layout;
   377:     IrTypeKind kind;

   365: typedef struct IrType IrType;
   366: struct IrType
   367: {
   368:     String8 name;
   369:     IrField* fields;
   370:     IrEnumMember* enum_members;
   371:     IrTypeId* parameter_types;
   372:     IrTypeId id;
   373:     IrTypeId element_type;
   374:     IrTypeId return_type;
   375:     IrTypeId unqualified_type;
   376:     IrTypeLayout layout;
   377:     IrTypeKind kind;
   378:     IrCallingConvention calling_convention;
   379:     u64 element_count;
   380:     u32 field_count;
   381:     u32 enum_member_count;
   382:     u32 parameter_count;
   383:     u32 bit_width;
   384:     bool is_signed;
   385:     bool is_variadic;
   386:     bool is_atomic;
   387:     bool is_nullptr;
   388:     bool is_volatile;
   389:     // GNU's transparent_union, carried from the C type so argument
   390:     // conversion can pass a member-typed value as the union's bits.  Only
   391:     // the C frontend sets and reads it.
   392:     bool is_transparent_union;
   393:     // A C complex type, modelled as the two-field struct the psABIs classify
   394:     // it as (see c_ir_complex_type). Only the C frontend sets and reads it;
   395:     // to the backends the type is an ordinary aggregate.
   396:     bool is_complex;
   397:     // A function type whose declarator spelled `noreturn`, so a call through
   398:     // it ends control flow. The marker written on a function declaration is
   399:     // read off that declaration instead (c_ir_declaration_is_noreturn); this
   400:     // carries the one shape no declaration can answer for -- the attribute on
   401:     // a function pointer type or typedef, where the call site only ever sees
   402:     // the type. Only the C frontend sets and reads it.
   403:     bool is_noreturn;
   404:     // A function type written `()` before C23: it declares no parameter list
   405:     // at all, so a call supplies the parameters itself from its own promoted
   406:     // argument types. The reference an IR_OPCODE_CALL takes to such a symbol
   407:     // therefore carries that call's own signature rather than the symbol's
   408:     // type (see the IR_OPCODE_FUNCTION rule in ir_validate_instruction), the
   409:     // way Clang gives `void die()` the type `void (...)` and each call site
   410:     // the type `void (i32, ...)`. `(void)` and C23's `()` are ordinary
   411:     // zero-parameter prototypes and never set it.
   412:     bool is_unprototyped;
   413:     u8 float_format;
   414: };

   465: // One symbol that is a second name for another: __attribute__((alias("t"))).
   466: // An alias owns no storage, no function body and no global initializer -- it
   467: // takes the target definition's section, offset and size and contributes only
   468: // its own binding -- so it is not a symbol property but a relation between
   469: // two, and it is kept as a module-level list of the pairs.  A module's
   470: // aliases must name definitions in that same module, which is what
   471: // ir_validate_canonical_module checks.
   472: typedef struct IrSymbolAlias IrSymbolAlias;
   473: struct IrSymbolAlias
   474: {
   475:     IrSymbolId symbol;
   476:     IrSymbolId target;
   477: };
   478:

   484:     u32 capacity;
   485: };
   486: 
   487: typedef struct IrSource IrSource;
   488: struct IrSource
   489: {
   490:     String8 path;
   491:     // The bytes an IrSourceRange offset indexes, for frontends that keep
   492:     // their sources whole and map them one-to-one. Empty when the program
   493:     // carries an IrSourceMap instead, which is the case whenever the byte
   494:     // space is not the file (the C frontend's preprocessing space holds
   495:     // every include's translated text and every synthesized spelling).
   496:     String8 text;
   497:     IrSourceId id;
   498: };
   499: 
   500: typedef struct IrSourceTable IrSourceTable;
   501: struct IrSourceTable
